Suppressor_Tank_Title	Maintenance Tank
Suppressor_Tank_Description	The Maintenance Tank is an autonomous drone that automatically maintains, manages, and repairs all devices and equipment developed by the Lamorn technicians in the Machine Era. These drones also function as a security force; if unauthorized individuals are detected in the facility, these drones are permitted to engage and eliminate intruders with their welding beam. Larger and smaller types have been developed to help carry out their various responsibilities.
Suppressor_Tank_Preview	{color[00ff9dff]}Mechanoid: Maintenance Tank{color[000000ff]}\nAutonomous mobile drone responsible for performing maintenance within the facility. Its welding beam can also be used against intruders.
Behemoth_Title	Behemoth
Behemoth_Description	The Behemoth is a giant arthropod that lives in the caves of Magdrahda Volcano. Its body is covered in a layer of mineralized skin that neutralizes all firearms, but its four eyes, which are directly connected to its brain via delicate nerves, are vulnerable to damage when open. It is aggressively territorial and will attack anyone that gets too close, either by rushing toward them in an attempt to ram them, or through a variety of fire-based attacks. These attacks are fueled by the Behemoth creating a flammable mixture of air and its own body fluids, which it can then emit either in a mist it ignites by striking the ground to create a fiery ring or as a series of blazing firebombs expelled from its three mouths.
Behemoth_Preview	{color[00ff9dff]}Arthropod Morphology: Behemoth{color[000000ff]}\nGiant arthropod with eight legs, four independent eyes, and three mouths. Its entire body is covered with a mineralized skin layer that neutralizes all firearms.\n\n\nAttacks by rapidly shooting flaming projectiles from its mouth, using oscillation waves to drive enemies away, and producing a powerful air suction to pull enemies in. Only its eyes are vulnerable to damage.
Pyremite_Title	Pyremite
Pyremite_Description	Pyremites are a type of single-eyed crustacean who crawl on eight large-clawed legs. They can be found in the Magdrahda Volcano. They are capable of creating an explosion from the top of their heads by mixing air with a flammable gas they produce inside of their bodies, compressing the mixture, then igniting it through rapid heat generation. If they detect intruders in their territory, they will launch out of the ground, rapidly approach the intruder, and then trigger an explosion. If they take critical damage, they can trigger a similar explosion by igniting any remaining gas within their body to self-destruct. This behavior is true even when a pyremite succumbs to illness or old age, resulting in few examples of pyremite remains.
Pyremite_Preview	{color[00ff9dff]}Arthropod Morphology: Pyremite{color[000000ff]}\nSees with a single, light-emitting eye and walks on\neight clawed feet. It will approach intruders in its\nterritory and trigger an explosion to repel them.
FP_KeyGuardian_Title	Phenoros
FP_KeyGuardian_Description	Phenoros is a giant mineral-based life-form that inhabits the lava lake of Magdrahda Volcano. It swims through and ingests lava, extracting and consuming any Green Energy present. A flexible organic membrane connects the many mineral segments that make up its body, which can grow to more than 150 meters in size. Metroid fusion has resulted in some dilation of the internal membrane, exposing it on the surface of the creature's body and leaving it vulnerable to damage. Destroying these exposed areas renders the creature immobile. It is able to use absorbed Green Energy to regenerate lost or damaged portions of its body, though this regenerative effect is limited in the short term. Its mineral segments are immune to all types of damage, but its tongue is organic and susceptible to attack. Damaging the tongue will incapacitate it, and it can then be fully defeated by destroying its internal core. 
FP_KeyGuardian_ControlBeamPhase_Preview	Phenoros is incapacitated after taking damage to its tongue. It will not recover if its core, which is down its throat and deep within its body, is destroyed.
FP_KeyGuardian_HeadPhase_Preview	Significant organic tissue mass—resulting from Metroid fusion—detected on Phenoros's head. Area vulnerable to damage. Damaging its tongue, where nerves are concentrated, may incapacitate Phenoros.
FP_KeyGuardian_Platform_Preview	Phenoros has temporarily lost motor control due to complete destruction of organic tissue on its abdomen. It appears unable to swim until it recovers. Estimated recovery time: unknown.

FP_SwarmFlyer_Title	Swarm Flyer
FP_SwarmFlyer_Description	Swarm Flyers live submerged in lava until they reach maturity, when their bodies begin generating a lighter-than-air gas that lifts them into the air. Once aloft, they join their peers, forming a swarm. Their propulsion is achieved by sucking air into the front of their body and expelling it from the rear, with trajectory adjustments made by redirecting the expulsion. Their internal gas dissipates after a week, and as their buoyancy fades, Swarm Flyers return to the place of their birth within the lava, spawn, and die.
FP_SwarmFlyer_Preview	{color[00ff9dff]}Aerial Morphology: Swarm Flyer{color[000000ff]}\nProduces a lighter-than-air gas in its body, then floats into the air. The organism then propels itself by inhaling and expelling air.
LavaJumper_Title	Blow Hopper
LavaJumper_Description	Blow Hoppers are floating organisms with heat-resistant properties, allowing them to inhabit lakes of lava like Flare Pool. They are able to navigate their environment by generating a lighter-than-air gas and inflating a pouch-like organ. They primarily consume Green Energy to survive, extracting it from the lava on Viewros by ingesting it, consuming the energy, then expelling the excess lava from their mouths. Due to their incredibly high metabolism and inefficient use of the consumed energy, they are perpetually in a cycle of consumption and expulsion.
LavaJumper_Preview	{color[00ff9dff]}Squamata Morphology: Blow Hopper{color[000000ff]}\nPossesses a body that produces gas lighter than the\nsurrounding atmosphere, allowing it to float in the air.\nIt absorbs energy from Viewros's lava.

LeapingSpitter_Description	Leaping Spitters are mollusks that inhabit Flare Pool. Normally they dive deep into the lava and remain inert, but if there are changes in their environments, such as dramatic shifts in the heat levels of the lava, they may temporarily migrate to more shallow areas of the pool. If they detect danger while in a shallow area, they will attempt to drive off the threat by flipping up into the air and scattering lava bombs around the area.

Carvex_Title	Carvex
Carvex_Description	A giant carnivorous plant that possesses self-awareness and cognitive thought. While it once coexisted and communicated with Lamorn priests using telepathic waves, one of the effects of Metroid fusion is a disruption in its thought pattern, preventing it from communicating. It now sees everyone as enemies and will attack indiscriminately.\n\nIt primarily attacks using its tentacles. These tentacles can be destroyed, but individual tentacles will grow back quickly through rapid cell proliferation and cell transfer from any remaining tentacles. If all of its tentacles are destroyed simultaneously, the regeneration process requires its body to generate considerable heat, forcing it to open its protective calyx to cool down. This will expose a thin mucous membrane that is vulnerable to damage.
Carvex_Preview	{color[00ff9dff]}Plant Morphology: Carvex{color[000000ff]}\nA giant carnivorous plant possessing consciousness, intelligence, and considerable physical strength. It is unable to communicate due to fusion with a Metroid.\n\n\nDestroying all tentacle-like vines will cause its protective leaves to briefly drop, exposing a body enveloped in only a thin mucous membrane.\n\n\n\nNote that the tentacles quickly regenerate. The only way to delay their regeneration is to destroy them all at virtually the same time.
Carvex_Seed_Title	Carvex's Pollen Sac
Carvex_Seed_Description	Carvex is capable of ejecting pouch-like pollen sacs at will. These sacks have four wings that function like propellers, allowing them to move slowly through the air and home in on targets detected using a natural thermal sensor. When a pollen sack lands on a target, the sack will rupture, causing damage. This behavior indicates that the sacks primarily serve as a means of attack rather than a method of pollination.
Carvex_Seed_Preview	{color[00ff9dff]}Plant Morphology: Carvex's Pollen Sac{color[000000ff]}\nPollen bags released from the ends of Carvex's tentacles. They possess propeller-like wings and fly by riding wind currents.
Carvex_Vine_Title	Carvex Vine
Carvex_Vine_Description	Carvex's tentacles are normally hidden beneath the ground, but it will reveal them if it detects danger. The tentacles have extreme elasticity, making them very flexible. This allows Carvex to attack by swinging them around like long whips. Swollen protuberances on the tentacles indicate the areas that facilitate Carvex's fine control of its limbs. If these are destroyed, the resulting loss of control will rapidly expand and rupture the affected tentacle.
Carvex_Vine_Preview	{color[00ff9dff]}Plant Morphology: Carvex Vine{color[000000ff]}\nTentacle-like appendage capable of being both rigid and flexible. It can be temporarily destroyed by damaging the bulging portion along its length.
Jungle_Griever_Title	Jungle Griever
Jungle_Griever_Description	A subspecies of "Griever"—the ferocious predators found throughout Viewros—which has adapted to densely wooded environments. Their exceptional jumping ability allows them to quickly traverse treetops and rock cliffs. They often hunt in packs and are known to attack other creatures indiscriminately. Once one pack member strikes, the others will quickly join the fray, either by leaping up close to slash with the sharp claws on their two pairs of arms or by spitting a viscous mass of venom from a distance. Their skin is thin and unarmored, making their bodies vulnerable to standard beam weaponry, and light enough that the shock of a direct hit from explosive weapons is enough to push them away.
Jungle_Griever_Preview	{color[00ff9dff]}Alien Morphology: Jungle Griever{color[000000ff]}\n"Griever" variant of this area. It can leap to great heights and climb trees quickly.\n\nIt has an extremely aggressive disposition and will use its arms and claws to attack its enemies at close range. When at a distance, it will spit poison at threats.
BarbFlinger_Title	Bristlewort
BarbFlinger_Description	Bristlewort are carnivorous plants capable of moving around using their six legs. Their outer shells are impervious to beam attacks, though explosives can damage them. These sturdy shells are heavy, impairing the Bristlewort's ability to move. As a result, they tend to attack from a distance, only opening the shell when prey is detected. Once the shell is open, Bristlewort quickly rotate, launching sharp thorns in all directions before closing to generate new thorns. Thanks to the mineral composition of the thorns, Bristlewort can regenerate them indefinitely using photosynthesis. Staying at a distance also helps them protect their internal organs, which are exposed and vulnerable even to beam attacks while the shell is open.
BarbFlinger_Preview	{color[00ff9dff]}Plant Morphology: Bristlewort{color[000000ff]}\nCarnivorous plant variant covered in a hard shell. When it senses a threat, it will open its shell, angle itself toward its target, and launch thorns.
Hopper_Title	Glottagropper
Hopper_Description	Glottagroppers are carnivorous reptiles who primarily feed on other animals. They are ambush predators, burrowing beneath the ground and waiting for prey to approach. When they sense the vibrations of prey through the ground, they will swiftly emerge to launch sharp needles from their mouth. They have two extremely powerful legs, which they can use to launch themselves into the air, up to three times their own height. They can then use their small wings to control their midair positioning, making it difficult for their prey to track their movements.
Hopper_Preview	{color[00ff9dff]}Reptile Morphology: Glottagropper{color[000000ff]}\nCarnivorous reptiles that move using wings and powerful legs. They burrow underground to wait for prey, then emerge when they detect vibrations.
Root_Lasher_Title	Rootspur
Root_Lasher_Description	Rootspur are tuberous root plants. They typically live below the water's surface in rivers and marshes or beneath the ground in bogs and will defend their territory from intruders. They have a thick, swollen stalk and also possess an autonomic nervous system. If they detect an invader, they will emerge from hiding and attack by swinging the vine on top of their body around themselves until the threat withdraws. The fleshy stalk is covered in hard bark, making it exceedingly durable, though explosives and high-powered weapons can still dispatch it quickly.
Root_Lasher_Preview	{color[00ff9dff]}Plant Morphology: Rootspur{color[000000ff]}\nA mobile, tuberous root plant variant with an autonomic\nnervous system. They usually live in mud underwater\nand will surface if a creature draws near.{[0004]}If their territory is invaded, they will attack with their\nvines until the intruder is defeated or driven away.\nDespite its hard outer skin, even a single shot from\na high-powered weapon can defeat it.
Spore_Launcher_Title	Ghaspore
Spore_Launcher_Description	Ghaspore are a type of mushroom that mainly inhabits hot and humid jungles, growing on surfaces like tree trunks or rock cliffs. Their periostracum remain closed until a sporangium is generated, at which point their periostracum opens and ejects the sporangium into the air. The periostracum is extremely hard and cannot be destroyed with beam weaponry, but the soft tissues beneath the periostracum—which are exposed when a sporangium is ejected—are vulnerable to damage from beams or other weapons. Ghaspore can be destroyed even while their periostracum is closed, using high-powered weapons.

Roaming_Spore_Title	Homing Spore
Roaming_Spore_Description	The sporangium that Ghaspore, a type of mushroom, eject at irregular intervals are called Homing Spores. They contain spores as well as a lighter-than-air gas mixture within their pouch-like structure. This gas allows Homing Spores to float in the air, waiting for creatures to approach. If they sense that a creature is nearby, they will grow thorny tactile receptors across their outer surface and begin pursuing their target. If they make contact with their target, the internal gases instantaneously compress while the outer layer heats up, causing the gas to explode.

Flying_Shooter_Title	Shren
Flying_Shooter_Description	Shren build their hives high off the ground, usually on rocky cliffs or against tree trunks. Both adult and larval Shren typically share a hive. If something approaches one of their hives, the adults will quickly emerge and protect the nest by spraying their acidic venom at the intruder. While they are highly aggressive when defending their hive, this behavior is defensive, and they will not pursue an intruder once it has withdrawn. In fact, Shren are social creatures with other Shren, often forming several hives in proximity to each other and working together to protect each other.

Mire_Worm_Title	Mireworm
Mire_Worm_Description	Mireworms are invertebrate mollusks that live in small groups, primarily in shallow freshwater and boggy areas. They possess long, slender bodies and will often emerge from their burrows in search of prey. They do this by swaying gently—appearing as vegetation—and emitting a sweet odor from their heads that mimics plants, luring in small insects. They use the mandibles at the tip of their head to grab their prey and then swallow them whole. They are highly vigilant, quickly retreating into their burrows if they sense danger. They are incapable of migration, spending their entire lives in their original burrows and dying if the water in their habitat is depleted.

Butterfly_Title	Empress Aleu
Butterfly_Description	Empress Aleu are a type of butterfly that have adapted to the heat of tropical climates. Their wings appear as a bright, vibrant blue, but this is not their actual color; it is the result of a complex phenomenon created by the way light wavelengths reflect off the scales covering the surface of their wings. When feeding, Empress Aleu form into small groups and seek out bryophytes and other small plants. Their bodies are relatively large compared to their wings, making them ill-suited to traveling long distances. As a result, they will often rest on rocks or trees, though they are quick to flee if they detect danger nearby.

They are quick to flee from enemies and predators, and they rely on their exceptional hearing to warn them if danger is nearby. Zant_Title	Red Relic Ant
Zant_Description	Red Relic Ants are arthropods that are often found in hot, humid regions. They prefer to create underground nests and typically live in groups of several dozen. Their bodies are red brown in color. They use the large pincers on the back of their torsos to both harvest the leaves of broadleaf trees and to carry their bounty back to their nest. They are known to travel great distances, sometimes up to several kilometers, in search of food. In order to find their way home, they will secrete pheromones from their abdomen, creating a trail they can follow using their olfactory senses. After collecting leaves and returning to the nest, they will shred the leaves and mix them with yeasted soil, ferment the combination, and then consume the highly nutritious result.

They possess an internal photophore and are known for their extremely docile disposition. They tend to gather in groups and share feeding grounds, using the mandibles on their head to peel away bark and harvest the sap beneath. A chemical reaction of photoproteins and enzymes allows them to emit light from the photophore in their abdomen. They use this luminescence as a means of communication within their groups, but when several of them are emitting light at once, it also makes them easy to spot by predators.

Horde_Griever_Title	Maul Griever
Horde_Griever_Description	Maul Grievers are Grievers that have adapted to the complex cave system of the Great Mines. They move quickly from area to area by burrowing through ground at high speeds using tough forearms and sharp, durable claws. Their sense of sight has faded over time due to their dark habitat, but a well-developed sense of hearing allows them to locate prey at great distances, even through solid stone. They reproduce in extreme numbers—greater than any other Griever variant—and will cooperate in order to make the most of this, often attempting to overwhelm threats by attacking in waves.\n\nIndividually, they are roughly as dangerous as other Grievers. However, their smooth skin, while ideal for traveling underground, also makes them more vulnerable to injury and death.

VF_Vermin_Title	Denzard
VF_Vermin_Description	Denzards are one-eyed arthropods with thin shells and a pair of forelegs they use for dragging their body.\n\nThe internal organs of a Denzard will not function without an electrical charge. Such a charge can be absorbed from their surroundings, however, which makes them well-adapted to life-averse environments such as Volt Forge, where even plants struggle to survive due to the constant harsh weather blocking all sunlight. Because Denzard organs circulate electricity, the cells in their eye, teeth, and tail glow.
VF_Vermin_Preview	{color[00ff9dff]}Arthropod Morphology: Denzard{color[000000ff]}\nA very resilient creature capable of living in incredibly harsh climates, such as Volt Forge. Can absorb electrical energy, causing parts of its body to glow.
VF_LightBotSwarm_Title	Mecha Rat
VF_LightBotSwarm_Description	A mechanoid variant with no entry in the\ncatalog of Lamorn-made creatures, Mecha\nRats are assumed to have been created by\nthe factory's Administration AI. Their bodies\nare powered by electricity and composed of\nthe same materials as a Vi-O-La. Being\nsmaller and lighter than Maintenance Tanks\nallows them to reach anywhere in the\nfacility, while cameras in their eyes capture\nvideo for the Administration AI to collect,\nview, and analyze.
VF_LightBotSwarm_Preview	Mechanoid variant. No entry found in the catalog of\nLamorn-made creatures. Analysis implies it was\nproduced by a powerful AI. Designation information\nis engraved on it, deciphered as "{color[00ff9dff]}Mecha Rat{color[000000ff]}."

Item_Bomb_Description	Psychic Bombs are weapons usable in Morph Ball mode, equal in power to the initial suit's Bombs. Up to three Psychic Bombs can be placed at a time. Bombs can also be infused with {color[ffc529ff]}Mote{color[000000ff]} properties by condensing the Psychic Energy within.\n\n{color[c2c2c2ff]}Activation{color[000000ff]}\nWhile in Morph Ball mode, press {[0100|57cd]} to place a Psychic Bomb.\nTo infuse a Psychic Bomb with Mote properties, press and hold\n{[0100|57cd]}. Once it is levitating, exit Morph Ball mode, switch to the\nPsychic Visor, and grasp the Bomb.\n\n{color[c2c2c2ff]}Mission Strategy{color[000000ff]}\nBombs damage enemies and can destroy structures that are weak to impacts, such as brittle walls. If a Bomb explodes in a {color[ffc529ff]}Bomb Slot{color[000000ff]}, the concussive blast will power any connected devices. Once infused with Mote properties, they can be used to reach Bomb Slots that are too high to reach in Morph Ball mode. By moving on top of a placed Bomb while in Morph Ball mode, its concussive blast can be used to jump.
Item_Ball_Description	The Morph Ball function of the Power Suit is used to transform into a sphere. This form enables access to certain pipes or narrow passages that are otherwise difficult to enter or traverse. Much like the Power Suit, the Morph Ball can be upgraded with additional functions.\n\n{color[c2c2c2ff]}Activation{color[000000ff]}\nPress {[0100|50cd]} to enter and exit Morph Ball mode.\n\n{color[c2c2c2ff]}Mission Strategy\n{color[000000ff]}Morph Ball mode allows entry into small openings and {color[ffc529ff]}tunnels{color[000000ff]}. The change in height can also be used to duck beneath some attacks or roll under enemies.
Item_BoostBall_Description	Boost Ball is an acceleration feature addition for Morph Ball\nmode that generates short bursts of high-speed rolling. This\ncan be used to power devices that support kinetic charging\nsuch as Spinners, for swift long-distance movement, and for\ntackling enemies.\n\n{color[c2c2c2ff]}Activation{color[000000ff]}\nWhile in Morph Ball mode, press and hold {[0100|59cd]} then release to\nunleash a surge of speed. Once in motion, immediately pressing,\nholding, and releasing {[0100|59cd]} can extend the speed surge.\n\n{color[c2c2c2ff]}Mission Strategy{color[000000ff]}\nBoost Ball can be used to shorten travel time, to avoid attacks\nduring battle, or to quickly flank enemies.
Item_ChargeBeam_Description	The Charge Beam condenses beam energy inside the Arm Cannon and releases it all at once for increased damage. When fully charged, it is five times stronger than a Power Beam shot.\n\n{color[c2c2c2ff]}Activation{color[000000ff]}\nHold {[0100|04cd]} to charge the beam, then release to fire.\n\n{color[c2c2c2ff]}Mission Strategy{color[000000ff]}\nCharging draws in nearby {color[ffc529ff]}energy and ammunition{color[000000ff]}. Shots may be launched half charged or fully charged, depending on the charge's duration. Half-charged shots are quicker but less powerful. Fully charged shots take longer to power up but inflict maximum damage.
Item_CombatVisor_Description	The Combat Visor is the suit's default vision mode. It displays remaining energy and missiles, a radar and minimap, and general information needed for combat and navigation.\n\n{color[c2c2c2ff]}Activation{color[000000ff]}\nIf there is a valid target in view, holding {[0100|09cd]} will {color[ffc529ff]}lock aim{color[000000ff]} on to the target nearest to your reticle. When in other visor modes, press {[0100|0dcd]} or {[0100|04cd]} to return to the Combat Visor.\n\n{color[c2c2c2ff]}Mission Strategy{color[000000ff]}\nThe {color[ffc529ff]}radar {color[000000ff]}in the top-left corner of the HUD shows the positions of both enemies and allies. AncientLore_Hologram_FG_Preview	In the distant past, a mass of burning rock fell from the\nsky to the jungles of Viewros. Once this rock, which\nwas itself poisonous, had cooled and hardened, a small\ncarnivorous plant took in the poison it carried.{[0004]}The plant did not succumb to the poison—it thrived,\ngrowing to rule over the jungle. In time, the priest\n"Thesarh" became the first the plant could convey\nits thoughts to and named it Carvex.{[0004]}Later, at their urging, Carvex eventually relocated to\nthe garden within the Lamorn Temple of Memory. There\nit ruled as it did in the jungles, and it came to preside\nover Lamorn funerals. 
AncientLore_Hologram_FP_Preview	It is unknown when this massive, snakelike creature\nfirst appeared in the lava lake of Magdrahda Volcano.\nWhat is known is that it is intelligent—capable even of\ncomplex thought—and calls itself Phenoros.{[0004]}Phenoros draws its strength from the consumption of\nthe lake's lava, is protected from danger thanks to a\nbody covered in solid rock, and breathes scorching\nflames from its mouth when threatened.{[0004]}Fortunately, Phenoros and the Lamorn have held a\nstrong friendship for a long time, and it was this\nfriendship that led to Phenoros allowing construction\nof the facility at the crater lake.
AncientLore_Hologram_GM_Preview	It is said that the creature originated from the deepest\nreaches of the land, that it first emerged in a cave far\naway from the light of the sun, and that it was the\npriest Thesarh who gave it the name Gravinax.{[0004]}Its huge body bore eight limbs, four sharp horns\ngrowing from its head, and a belly and back that were\ncovered in thick skin.{[0004]}It used its horns to burrow through rock, propelling\nitself forward in a spin using its sturdy limbs, leaving\nnumerous stones in its wake. These stones would\nprove a source of nourishment for the Lamorn.
AncientLore_Hologram_IB_Preview	It lived beyond thick ice, where frozen shards danced\non gale winds, and frigid breath met any approach. In its mind was intolerance, living isolated and intemperate\nlike an aloof warrior. It called itself Keratos.{[0004]}His nature was that of violence, and at the peak of his\nfury, Keratos was known to throw his giant body at\nany who might stoke his rage, or spin so quickly that\nicy pillars would form.{[0004]}It was through communion with Thesarh that a warmth\ntook root in his heart. Slowly, his rage subsided, and he\nbecame more taciturn. In time, this calmer Keratos\nwould be hailed as a guardian deity of the Lamorn.
AncientLore_Hologram_VF_Preview	Not all Lamorn controlled power from within. In a place\nwhere dark clouds never clear, endless lightning slices\nthe sky, and the wind and rain rage, our forebears laid\nthe foundations for the Machine Era.{[0004]}Here, three giant towers were built, piercing the dark\nsky. These would become the birthplace of a tool able\nto race across the land on spinning wheels.{[0004]}But this harsh environment proved unsuitable for the\nLamorn peoples. This was why Lamorn technologists\ncreated an artificial life-form to oversee the towers.\nThey named this life-form Xelios.{[0004]}Its singular directive was "to seek the happiness of the\nLamorn." With command of the electricity flowing\nthrough the towers and the machines connected to\nthem, it could follow its directive as it chose.{[0004]}Even now, with the Machine Era long since passed,\nXelios seeks the happiness of the Lamorn.

However, at the\nguidance of Priest Chatoya, a few of us\nstayed in order to perform analysis of, and\nexperiments on, Griever anatomy.\n\nOur hope is to reverse the transformation\nby administering a solution of processed\nGreen Energy to subjects brought to the\nfacility. I want to believe we can succeed.

IB_ResearchLog_03_Title	"Security"

Operation of the cooling equipment\nhas been deemed absolutely essential, so\nthe system has been designed to continue\nfunctioning even if the entire facility were to\nfall into low-power mode. Similarly, thorough\ninspection and repair of Cryopods is one of\nour highest-priority tasks.

IB_ResearchLog_04_Description	LMH: 20062001083\nGreen Energy dosage results show no\nsignificant benefits at this time. Essentially, a\nrapid increase in cellular proliferation speed\nand a doubling of the number of cells\nincreases growth and affects vitality. But\nwhy has this vector taken such an abnormal\nturn, and what can be done to shift it back to\nits original direction? Our time is limited, and\nI'm worried about our remaining stockpiles.

IB_ResearchLog_05_Title	"Awakening"
IB_ResearchLog_05_Description	LMH: 20062001107\nThe subject is in hibernation in the Cryopod,\nand its metabolism is at minimum levels.\nUnfortunately, we are unable to perform the\nexperiments we'd like with the subject in this\nstate. If the subject's metabolic function is\ninactive, a reversal of its rapid cellular\nproliferation cannot occur.\n\nFirst, we must wake the subject and restore\nits normal metabolic function. This also\nmeans restoration of consciousness, so\nappropriate sedation will be crucial.

IB_ResearchLog_06_Title	"Analysis"
IB_ResearchLog_06_Description	LMH: 20062001123\nWe are monitoring subjects who have had\nGE administered here. Regardless of GE\nconcentration, there do not appear to be any\nsignificant differences. We see regeneration\nwithout an apparent mutation to alter the\ncells. Furthermore, no matter how deep our\nanalysis, we have yet to see any apparent\nchromosome abnormalities—it is almost\nas if they are natural creatures. Put another\nway, if we were to judge based on DNA,\nthere are no significant differences between\nthem and us. What is going on?
IB_ResearchLog_06_Preview	Accessing encrypted file.\nResearch Diary Day 97: "Analysis"\nData downloaded to Logbook.
IB_ResearchLog_07_Title	"Breakthrough"
IB_ResearchLog_07_Description	LMH: 20062001143\nWe may have had a breakthrough today.\nUntil now, we presumed that the physical\ntransformation was triggered by something\nin the crystals causing compositional change\nin the GE, which would in turn cause an\nabnormality in gene transcription. Yet that is\nnot the case. In actuality, an unused region\nof the brain linked with the crystals is\nstimulated and, as a result, genes that\nwould normally have stayed pseudogenes\nwere transcribed... I now wonder. Are we\nunlocking a blocked evolutionary branch?\n\nThis would mean "the Great Tragedy" was\nmerely one possible evolution of one\npotential future. So, with that in mind, what\nwould happen if, when administering GE, we\ndisabled those genes at the moment that the\nrapid cellular proliferation is triggered?\nThere should be sufficient value in\nattempting this to justify altering the\nBalsallae System's programming.
IB_ResearchLog_07_Preview	Accessing encrypted file.\nResearch Diary Day 117: "Breakthrough"\nData downloaded to Logbook.
IB_ResearchLog_08_Title	"Attempt"
IB_ResearchLog_08_Description	LMH: 20062001164\nWe were so close! It seemed like we had\nsucceeded in disabling the problematic\ngenes—and also resetting the cells—causing\nthe rapid-proliferation process to replicate\nnormal genetic information and restore their\nstate. However, for some reason, we were\nunable to stabilize it.\n\nThe subjects reverted.\n\nRight now, I want to check over the genetic\nanalysis to see if there were any problems in\nour process. I do not want this setback to\nhave been for nothing.

Viola_Title	Vi-O-La
Viola_Description	The Vi-O-La was originally a two-wheeled, single-seater vehicle developed during the Machine Era.\n\nAs part of a Psychic Era redesign, a function was added for identifying the Psychic brain waves of individual Lamorn. When a rider wearing a special nanosuit registered themselves to a Vi-O-La, they would be set as its exclusive rider and prevent its use by other Lamorn.\n\nThis registration allowed riders to summon their Vi-O-La to them, even from remote locations. However, summoning would fail if the route crossed impassible terrain.\n\nThe Vi-O-La's features also include pre-installed, unlockable functions. For instance, upgrading the Vi-O-La Suit to the Vi-O-La IC Suit enables the vehicle's IC mode, a hover transformation for moving over hot surfaces unsuitable for tires. Supporting this feature are heat-resistance systems strong enough to allow for hovering over magma and lava. This transformation would be automatically triggered by the vehicle's surface sensors.\n\nFurther transformation features were in the planning stages.
